
we_frontier_access = {
    OR = { we_frontier_coastal_access = { COLONIST = $COLONIST$ }
    any_neighboring_county = {
        exists = holder
        holder = { OR = { this = $COLONIST$ target_is_liege_or_above = $COLONIST$ } }
    } }
}
we_frontier_available = {
    has_variable = we_frontier_stage
    var:we_frontier_stage = 0
    exists = holder
    holder = { government_has_flag = we_frontier_wilderness }
    we_frontier_access = { COLONIST = $COLONIST$ }
}
we_frontier_sponsor_ready = {
    is_alive = yes is_adult = yes is_landed = yes is_imprisoned = no is_at_war = no
    NOT = { government_has_flag = we_frontier_wilderness }
    NOT = { has_character_flag = we_frontier_cooldown }
    NOT = {
        any_in_global_list = {
            list = we_frontier_titles
            OR = { var:we_frontier_stage = 1 var:we_frontier_stage = 2 }
            exists = var:we_frontier_sponsor_title.holder
            var:we_frontier_sponsor_title.holder = prev
        }
    }
    trigger_if = {
        limit = { is_ai = yes }
        is_independent_ruler = yes realm_size >= 5 gold >= 500
    }
}
